				google_tag_params = {
					edu_pid: "",
					edu_plocid: "Australia",
					edu_pagetype: ""
				};
			

			
				
				
				
				
				
				
				
				google_tag_params['function'] = location.pathname.startsWith('/lp/') ? 'landing' : 'general';
				google_tag_params['type'] = 'home';

				google_tag_params['sessionID'] = '3db9bda6-4d90-426c-a05c-3675e07cc987';
				var width = $(window).width();
				if (width <= 720) var device = 'M';
				else if (width <= 980) var device = 'T';
				else var device = 'D';
				google_tag_params['Device'] = device;

				/* opti  stuff starts here */
				/* Read Create Delete Cookie functions */
				function createCookie(name,value,days) {
					if (days) {
						var date = new Date();
						date.setTime(date.getTime()+(days*24*60*60*1000));
						var expires = "; expires="+date.toGMTString();
					}
					else var expires = "";
					document.cookie = name+"="+value+expires+"; path=/";
				}

				function readCookie(name) {
					var nameEQ = name + "=";
					var ca = document.cookie.split(';');
					for(var i=0;i < ca.length;i++) {
						var c = ca[i];
						while (c.charAt(0)==' ') c = c.substring(1,c.length);
						if (c.indexOf(nameEQ) == 0) return c.substring(nameEQ.length,c.length);
					}
					return null;
				}

				function eraseCookie(name) {
					createCookie(name,"",-1);
				}
				function getParameterByName(name, url) {
					if (!url) {
					  url = window.location.href;
					}
					name = name.replace(/[\[\]]/g, "\\$&");
					var regex = new RegExp("[?&]" + name + "(=([^&#]*)|&|#|$)"),
						results = regex.exec(url);
					if (!results) return null;
					if (!results[2]) return '';
					return decodeURIComponent(results[2].replace(/\+/g, " "));
				}
				function CRUDCookie(cookieName, cookieValue){
					if(readCookie(cookieName)){
						//console.log('cookie exists');
						//console.log(cookieName);
						var cookieTemp = '';
						if(!cookieName.match(cookieValue)){
							//console.log('updating cookie');
							cookieTemp = readCookie(cookieName);
                            if (cookieTemp.indexOf(cookieValue) == -1){
							    eraseCookie(cookieName);
							    createCookie(cookieName,cookieTemp+ ", " +cookieValue,30);
                            }
						}else {
							//console.log(cookieValue+ ' already exists cookie');
						}
					}else{
						//console.log('creating new cookie ' +cookieName);
						createCookie(cookieName,cookieValue,30);
					}
				}
				/* end  */

				/* OPTI and DL  vars */

				/* course code */
				var OPTI = 'cecode';
				if(location.search.match('enquiry_sent')){
					var courseCode = getParameterByName('code');
				} else {
					var courseCode = '';
				}

				/* Area Code */
				var OPTIAreaCode = 'ceAreaCode';
				
					var leadAreaCode = '';
				

				if(getParameterByName('code') && location.search.match('enquiry_sent')){
					CRUDCookie(OPTI, courseCode);
					CRUDCookie(OPTIAreaCode, leadAreaCode);

					google_tag_params[OPTI] = readCookie(OPTI);
					google_tag_params[OPTIAreaCode] = readCookie(OPTIAreaCode);
				}

				/* end OPTI stuff */

					$('#form-1 [name=area]').change(function() {
						var form = $(this.form);


						form.find('[name=00NE0000000ma2v]').empty();
						if (form.find('[name=area]').val() == '') {
							form.find('[name=00NE0000000ma2v]').append('<option value="">\u002D\u002D Select Area \u002D\u002D</option>');
						} else {
							form.find('[name=00NE0000000ma2v]').append('<option value="">\u002D\u002D Select Course \u002D\u002D</option>');
							cs = lookup[form.find('[name=area]').val()]['courses'];
							for (c in cs) {
								if (!cs.hasOwnProperty(c)) continue;
								c = cs[c];
								var o = $('<option></option>');
								o.val(c[0]).text(c[1]);
								form.find('[name=00NE0000000ma2v]').append(o);
							}

<div aria-labelledby="CareerCommitmentModal" class="modal fade" id="CareerCommitmentModal" role="dialog" tabindex="">
<div class="modal-dialog" role="document">
<div class="modal-content">
<div class="modal-header">
<button aria-label="Close" class="close" data-dismiss="modal" type="button"><span aria-hidden="true">×</span></button>
<img src="/static/oca/377787cc1af83524a6f5547befb1620efb29876b/oca4/base/img/career-commitment/OC_career-commitment-logo.svg"/>
<h4 class="modal-title">Career Commitment T&amp;C's</h4>
</div>
<div class="modal-body text-left">
<ol>
<li>This commitment enables Open Colleges students who do not achieve the objective specified during their enrolment (e.g. Enter the workforce, career change, promotion, learning new skills/hobby) within 6 months of completing their course, to receive a second Open Colleges course or module selected by Open Colleges of equal or lesser value for no additional course fee (the <strong>Commitment Course</strong>), in order to assist the student to gain additional training necessary to achieve their specified outcome.</li>
<li>The conditions of the commitment are:
	<ol class="alpha-list">
<li>The Commitment only applies to online courses offered by Open Colleges</li>
<li>The Commitment does not apply to face-to-face courses offered by the School of Health</li>
<li>It only applies to Australian domestic students.</li>
<li>The student must have successfully completed or graduated from their initial course within the maximum course duration</li>
<li>The commitment will only apply if the student has paid their course fees in full</li>
<li>If course pre-requisites and/or eligibility criteria apply for the Commitment Course, the student must meet these conditions to be enrolled in the Commitment Course</li>
<li>The commitment will only apply if the student has made reasonable efforts to achieve their specified objective during their course and within the 6 months’ post course completion. Open Colleges will assess and determine whether the student has made reasonable efforts based on evidence of effort provided by the student. <strong>Examples of this include:</strong>
<ul>
<li>Proof of job applications in an industry related to their course of study</li>
<li>Proof of applications within the same employer for promotion or other jobs related to their course of study</li>
<li>Students that enrol as a hobbyist will need to let us know what skills were not learned in their course (to help us close the skill gap)</li>
</ul>
</li>
<li>To accept the commitment, the student must contact Open Colleges within 6 months after the date which is 6 months after completion date of the initial course, after which the commitment expires.</li>
<li>The commitment applies to any course of equal or lesser value. If the second course is more expensive the student will need to pay the difference in the course fees.</li>
<li>The offer is limited to one Commitment Course</li>
<li>The commitment is non-transferrable</li>
<li>The commitment is only valid for new enrolments after (July 3rd, 2017).</li>
<li>Open Colleges reserves the right to cease to offer the commitment at any time without notice, provided that the commitment will remain in place for any student enrolled prior to the commitment offer being cancelled.</li>
</ol>
</li>
</ol>
</div>
<div class="modal-footer">
<button class="btn btn-default" data-dismiss="modal" type="button">Close</button>
</div>
</div><!-- /.modal-content -->
</div><!-- /.modal-dialog -->
</div><!-- /.modal -->

Offer excludes Open Colleges School of Health courses, ED2Go courses, Short Courses and Learning Library subscriptions. Any courses without payment plans are also not eligible for this offer. <h2>Course Areas</h2>
<h5>Open Colleges now offers blended on-campus options for our nursing, health and care courses in South Australia and Western Australia. With state funding available, organised work placements, great student support and outstanding job outcomes these courses are fantastic options to start or build your career in Australia’s health sector.</h5>

<script type="text/javascript">
				function getCookieValue(ckie, nme) {
					var splitValues;
					for (var i = 0; i < ckie.length; i++) {
						splitValues = ckie[i].split("=");
						var key = splitValues[0]
						key = key.replace(/^\s*/gi, "");
						if (key == nme) {
						  return splitValues[1];
						}
					}
					return 0;
				}
				function counterCookie() {
					  var adName = "rfiAdServeCountY";
					  var num_of_hours_to_save_cookie = 3;
					  var cookie = document.cookie;
					  var counter = 0;
					  var nvpair = cookie.split(";");
					  var checkCookie = parseInt(getCookieValue(nvpair,adName), 10);

					if (checkCookie != 0 || checkCookie != "") {
						counter = checkCookie;
					}

					  counter++;
					  var newCookieDate = new Date();
					  newCookieDate.setTime(newCookieDate.getTime()+(num_of_hours_to_save_cookie*60*60*1000));
					  var newCookie = adName + "=" + counter;
					  newCookie += "; expires=" + newCookieDate.toGMTString() + "; path=/";
					  window.document.cookie = newCookie;
					  return counter;
				}

				(function () {
					var count = counterCookie();
					var num_of_times_before_firing_conversion = 2;
					var protocol = "https:" == document.location.protocol ? 'https:' : 'http:';
					new Image(1,1).src = protocol+"//p.rfihub.com/ca.gif?rb=20233&ca=20719595&ra="+Math.random()+"&ud="+count;
					if (count == num_of_times_before_firing_conversion) {
						new Image(1,1).src = protocol+"//p.rfihub.com/ca.gif?rb=20233&ca=20719596&ra="+Math.random()+"&ud="+count;
					}
				})();
